# Apple Watch — Review Index

Written by Claude (Claude Code), 10 October 2026. Companion to [Apple Watch App — What People Want, What Breaks, and How Ours Works](<../Apple Watch App — What People Want, What Breaks, and How Ours Works.md>).
Every App Store review naming a watch (3,051, from 337,331 reviews of 74 habit and routine apps), read one by one and coded by hand. Each entry: app, stars, review ID. A review can carry several codes. The map is `codes.py` (keyed by reading-list row); `make_index.py` checks every review was coded exactly once.

## W — wants a Watch app (the app has none, or the person can't find it) (764)
